### [1] [Ankhdjet: An Open-Source Compiler for Mask-Programmed Ternary Compute-in-ROM on an Open PDK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26206)
*Mohnish Pai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个开源的权重到掩码的ROM计算编译器，可在开放PDK上实现大语言模型的三值量化硬件加速，并已提交流片验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理的主要瓶颈是权重移动：每生成一个token都要重新读取所有权重。三值量化(BitNet b1.58)将每个权重量化至1.58比特，在20亿参数规模下达到性能对等，这使将权重硬连线到只读掩码成为可能。本文探索是否可以用完全开放的基础设施实现模型专用硅片的可复现性。

Method: 开发了Ankhdjet开源编译器，将HuggingFace三值检查点(BitNet b1.58及其同类)转换为开放SKY130 PDK上固定ROM计算宏的掩码程序。采用全数字读取方案（全摆幅位线采样到标准单元），相比模拟比较器读取方案面积减少至八分之一。通过完整的开源工具链验证，包括KLayout DRC、netgen LVS和时序检查。

Result: 实现了首个在可制造开放PDK上的开源权重到掩码ROM计算编译器，完成了完整的开源工具链签核（DRC为零、LVS为零、时序干净），使用两个不同权重矩阵通过相同流程验证。已提交首个在开放PDK上进行流片的ROM计算宏（TinyTapeout/ChipFoundry SKY130 shuttle ttsky26c，预计2027年获得硅片）。读取能耗为0.98-1.73 pJ/感知权重（基于提取寄生参数仿真）。

Conclusion: 证明了使用完全开放的基础设施实现模型专用硅片的可行性，为三值量化大语言模型的硬件加速提供了可复现的开源解决方案。同时报告了对抗性验证方法论、在130nm工艺下每个晶体管存储两个三值权重的负面结果，以及不同工艺节点下模拟感知技术的可行性分析。

Abstract: Large-language-model inference is dominated by weight movement: every generated token re-reads every weight. Ternary quantization (BitNet b1.58) shrinks each weight to 1.58 bits with reported parity at the 2B-parameter scale, small enough that hardwiring the weights into a read-only mask becomes plausible, and a commercial chip (Taalas HC1) has validated hardwired weights on an advanced node behind closed tooling. This paper asks whether model-specific silicon can be made reproducible with entirely open infrastructure. We present Ankhdjet, an open-source compiler that lowers a HuggingFace ternary checkpoint (BitNet b1.58 and its kin) to a via-mask program of a fixed compute-in-ROM macro on the open SKY130 PDK, verified end to end with open tools. We defend two claims: (1) the first open-source weights-to-mask compute-in-ROM compiler on a fabricable open PDK, taken through full open-toolchain signoff (KLayout DRC zero, netgen LVS zero, clean timing) twice with two different weight matrices through an identical flow in which only the mask differs; and (2) the first compute-in-ROM macro submitted for fabrication on an open PDK (TinyTapeout/ChipFoundry SKY130 shuttle ttsky26c, silicon expected 2027). The compiler's read is fully digital (full-swing bitline sampling into standard cells, so the emission ports to any node by synthesis); an analog comparator readout of the same machine is retained as a measured variant, replaced by the digital readout at one eighth the area. We additionally report the per-level adversarial verification methodology that caught three DRC-invisible silent shorts, a negative result on storing two ternary weights per transistor at 130 nm, and which analog sense techniques survive at which nodes. Read energy is from extracted-parasitic simulation (0.98-1.73 pJ per sensed weight); no energy measurements are claimed, and the fabricated vehicles are demonstrators.

</details>


### [2] [MeshReduce-U: Compiler-Guided Communication Reduction for Irregular Neural Reductions on Mesh NoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26220)
*Amirreza Khorasanian*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MeshReduce-U是一种面向网格NoC空间加速器的编译器指导通信缩减与路由框架，通过在路由前重写可缩减的神经通信，在20个工作负载上平均降低延迟40.3%、总链路使用量56.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多不规则神经网络工作负载产生偏斜的多对一归约操作，具有重复邻域和非局部通信特征。传统NoC映射器针对固定通信图优化布局与路由，却忽略了结合律归约操作在路由前消除通信流量的合法机会。

Method: MeshReduce-U框架实施五项关键技术：合并共置源节点、形成局部聚合岛屿、阻塞具有兼容扇入结构的通道、选择容量可行的汇点，并使用融合使用感知成本路由剩余固定宽度载波。采用确定性路由-重放模型报告调度导出的通信延迟、总链路使用量（TLU）和融合链路使用量（FusedTLU）。

Result: 在20个可降低神经网络工作负载上，相对于ABC风格源聚合基线，MeshReduce-U分别降低平均延迟、TLU和FusedTLU 40.3%、56.0%和48.7%，每个工作负载三项指标均有提升。在40个合成不规则归约上，平均延迟和TLU分别降低12.3%和19.7%。30实例逐遍研究显示，结构重写将全局载波计数降低60.9%，重放延迟降低63.0%。

Conclusion: 在路由前重写可缩减神经通信比在未缩减流量图上更努力搜索更为有效，证明了通信缩减优先于路由优化的优越性。

Abstract: Many irregular neural workloads induce skewed many-to-one reductions with repeated neighborhoods and nonlocal communication. Conventional NoC mappers optimize placement and routes for a fixed communication graph, even though associative reductions expose legal opportunities to eliminate traffic before routing. We present MeshReduce-U, a compiler-guided communication-reduction and routing framework for mesh-NoC-based spatial accelerators.
  MeshReduce-U coalesces colocated sources, forms local aggregation islands, blocks channels with compatible fan-in structure, selects capacity-feasible sinks, and routes the remaining fixed-width carriers using fused usage-aware costs. A deterministic route-replay model reports schedule-derived communication latency, total link usage (TLU), and fused link usage (FusedTLU) separately.
  Across a 20-workload lowerable neural-network zoo, MeshReduce-U reduces mean latency, TLU, and FusedTLU by 40.3%, 56.0%, and 48.7%, respectively, relative to an ABC-style source-aggregation baseline, improving all three metrics on every workload. Across 40 synthetic irregular reductions, it reduces mean latency and TLU by 12.3% and 19.7%. A new 30-instance pass-by-pass study further shows that the structural rewrites reduce the global carrier count by 60.9% and replay latency by 63.0%. These results show that rewriting reducible neural communication before routing can be more effective than searching harder over an unreduced traffic graph.

</details>


### [3] [Beyond Edge Cuts: Activity-Weighted Multicast Hypergraph Mapping for Spiking Neural Networks on Mesh NoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26223)
*Amirreza Khorasanian*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出M-HySMap框架，通过路由感知的超图映射方法优化SNN在神经形态多核平台上的部署，相比现有方法减少19.7-41.1%的路由跳数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN映射方法使用图划分和成对放置成本抽象，但这与物理通信事件不匹配。实际通信中，一个源神经元的脉冲会传递给多个突触后目标，且到不同目标的路由可以共享mesh链路。传统抽象忽略了这种多播特性和路由共享，导致映射效果不佳。

Method: 提出M-HySMap路由感知、活动加权的多播超图映射框架。核心方法包括：(1)为每个源神经元创建指向其突触后输出的有向超边，权重基于活动分析；(2)从强活动感知的图/QAP种子开始优化目标核心输出、确定性mesh路由集合和链路拥塞；(3)利用局部性特性实现精确增量增益评估，即移动一个神经元仅影响其自身超边和前驱超边；(4)设计保守的放置下界和保持最优解的分区与放置邻域组合。

Result: 在115个测试作业上验证（基于Potjans启发式循环SNN和4x4到6x6 mesh NoC，外加7x7压力测试），M-HySMap相比Activity+QAP减少10.6-19.6%的路由多播跳数，相比Edge+QAP减少19.7-41.1%。增量更新使优化速度提升4.7-12.7倍，且与完全重计算精度一致。

Conclusion: M-HySMap成功解决了传统SNN映射方法与物理通信事件不匹配的问题，通过超图建模和增量优化显著降低了路由开销，为神经形态计算平台的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mapping spiking neural networks (SNNs) onto neuromorphic many-core platforms is often formulated with graph partitioning and pairwise placement costs. That abstraction is convenient, but it does not match the physical communication event: one spike from a source neuron is delivered to a set of postsynaptic destinations, and routes to several destinations can share mesh links. We present M-HySMap, a route-aware, activity-weighted multicast hypergraph mapping framework. Each source neuron induces a directed hyperedge to its postsynaptic fanout, weighted by profiled activity. The mapper starts from strong activity-aware graph/QAP seeds and then optimizes distinct destination-core fanout, the union of deterministic mesh routes, and link congestion. The central algorithmic observation is locality: moving one neuron can change only its own source-rooted hyperedge and the hyperedges of its predecessors. This permits exact incremental gain evaluation while caching every unaffected route contribution. We expose this combinatorial structure in detail, derive a conservative placement lower bound, and describe a portfolio of partition and placement neighborhoods that preserves the best incumbent. Across a 115-job evidence suite on Potjans-inspired recurrent SNNs and mesh NoCs from 4 x 4 to 6 x 6, plus a 7 x 7 stress case, M-HySMap reduces routed multicast hops by 10.6-19.6% over Activity+QAP and 19.7-41.1% over Edge+QAP. Incremental updates accelerate refinement by 4.7-12.7x while matching full recomputation to numerical precision.

</details>


### [4] [Redwood: A Frontier AI Accelerator Designed, Verified, and Deployed from Scratch in 2 Weeks by AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26418)
*Architect Labs*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个由AI系统端到端设计的生产级AI加速器Redwood，实现软硬件协同优化，两周内完成从高层规范到RTL设计、验证和部署的全流程，在Samsung 8nm工艺下相比Jetson Orin Nano实现3.4倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI工作负载与硬件演进时间尺度不匹配：架构定义领先硅片量产数年，而目标工作负载数月即变。设计决策在深度不确定性下做出，需支付双重代价——通用性设计作为对冲，以及新工作负载在固化硅片上映射不佳。随着摩尔定律停滞，专业化成为性能功耗比的主要来源，亟需与工作负载节奏同步的设计周期。

Method: 构建端到端AI系统，将软件到硅片栈折叠为单一优化循环，在统一目标下实现软硬件协同设计与验证。系统从两位人类架构师提供的高层规范出发，自主生成性能模型、RTL设计、UVM验证环境、形式化证明、固件和内核。采用商用EDA工具、自研形式化引擎和硬件在环验证，全流程无人工干预。规范变更可在48小时内重新验证并部署到硬件。

Result: Redwood加速器面向物理AI的单批次、低功耗、超低延迟推理。各模块达95%覆盖率。FPGA变体Redwood Nano可运行Llama和Qwen等数十亿参数模型。在Samsung 8nm工艺下（与Jetson Orin Nano同级），相比实测Jetson基线，吞吐量提升1.75倍，功耗降低1.9倍，能效提升3.4倍。Qwen模型在Redwood上运行并参与下一代Redwood设计，初步实现递归自我改进。

Conclusion: 这是首个由AI系统端到端设计并运行现代AI模型的生产级AI加速器，验证了AI驱动的硬件设计自动化可行性，显著缩短设计周期，为实现工作负载与硬件设计节奏同步开辟新途径。

Abstract: Modern AI workloads and the hardware that runs them evolve on different timescales: architectural definition precedes volume silicon by years, while target workloads shift in months. Design decisions are therefore committed under deep uncertainty and paid for twice, once in the generality added as a hedge, and again when new workloads map poorly onto frozen silicon. As Moore's Law stagnates, specialization is the main remaining source of performance-per-watt and demands a design cycle that runs at the cadence of the workloads. We present an end-to-end AI system that collapses the software-to-silicon stack into a single optimization loop, where hardware and software are co-designed and verified under one objective. Its first demonstration is Redwood, a frontier AI accelerator built for single-batch, low-power, ultra-low-latency inference for physical AI. From a high-level specification by two human architects, the system autonomously generated the performance model, RTL design, UVM environments, formal proofs, firmware, and kernels in under two weeks with no human intervention below the specification. Every block reached 95% coverage via commercial EDA tools, our proprietary formal engine, and hardware-in-the-loop validation. Specification changes were reverified and redeployed to hardware in under 48 hours. Redwood Nano, its ultra-low-power FPGA variant, runs multi-billion-parameter models like Llama and Qwen. Projected onto Samsung 8 nm, the Jetson Orin Nano's process class, Redwood delivers 1.75x the throughput at 1.9x lower power, a 3.4x performance-per-watt gain against a measured Jetson baseline on the same models. Qwen running on Redwood also helped design next-generation Redwood, an early step toward recursive self-improvement. To our knowledge, this is the first production-worthy AI accelerator designed end-to-end by an AI system and running a modern AI model.

</details>


### [5] [HOLMES: In-Context Failure-Center Localization for High-Dimensional Yield Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26758)
*Wei W. Xing,Xixi Zhou,Kaiqi Huang,Jiaye Pan,Hong Qiu,Xin Wang,Shan Shen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HOLMES方法，利用表格基础模型的上下文学习解决高维高σ良率估计中的失效中心定位问题，在6T SRAM基准测试中实现5.9%以内相对误差和最高58.8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高σ良率估计需要在极度不平衡样本集中定位失效中心。现有基于代理的方法依赖迭代梯度训练，在极端类别不平衡下不适定；模型误差传播到估计器中，导致高维情况下精度崩溃。

Method: 将失效中心定位重构为少样本二元分类问题：使用预训练表格基础模型进行无梯度上下文推理（单次前向传播）；结合基于SVD的各向异性提议捕获失效流形局部几何；采用命中率驱动的自适应混合方案稳定重要性权重。

Result: 在108至1152维的6T SRAM基准测试中，全维基线方法在某些维度出现精度崩溃，最强基线相对误差达25.8%；HOLMES在所有五种配置下相对误差保持在5.9%以内，相比蒙特卡洛实现最高58.8倍加速。

Conclusion: HOLMES成功解决了高维高σ良率估计中的失效中心定位难题，通过消除不适定训练循环并引入各向异性提议和自适应混合机制，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: Importance sampling for high-sigma yield estimation requires locating the failure center from a severely imbalanced sample set. Existing surrogate-assisted methods rely on iterative gradient-based training, ill-posed under extreme class imbalance; model errors propagate into the estimator, causing accuracy collapse in high dimensions. We recast failure-center localization as few-shot binary classification: a prior-fitted tabular foundation model performs gradient-free in-context inference in a single forward pass, eliminating the ill-posed training loop. \textbf{HOLMES} (High-sigma Optimal Localization via Manifold Estimation and Sampling) pairs this with an SVD-based anisotropic proposal that captures the local geometry of the failure manifold, and a hit-rate-driven adaptive mixing scheme that stabilizes importance weights where conventional adaptation collapses. On 6T SRAM benchmarks spanning $D = 108$ to $D = 1{,}152$, full-dimensional baselines exhibit accuracy collapse at some dimension, with the strongest baseline reaching 25.8\% relative error; PCA+MNIS is additionally evaluated at the two largest dimensions. HOLMES remains within 5.9\% across all five configurations with up to $58.8\times$ speedup over Monte Carlo. The code is available on \href{https://github.com/IceLab-JCIE/ICE006-Yield-Holmes}

</details>


### [6] [LLMs in Digital EDA: A perspective on shifting roles from Generation to Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27184)
*Matthew Youngman,Cristian Sestito,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM在EDA中的三级角色框架（生成器、代理、协调器），揭示了当前方法陷入"语法陷阱"且难以扩展到工业级设计的问题，主张构建物理感知的标准化协调器以实现更可靠的硬件设计自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EDA文献中LLM解决方案多局限于孤立设计阶段，掩盖了能力涌现和系统扩展的驱动因素；需要系统性框架揭示LLM能力如何在EDA流程中积累与扩展。

Method: 定义LLM在EDA中的三级层次化角色：生成器（单次产生设计制品）、代理（通过迭代工具反馈优化输出）、协调器（跨EDA阶段协调决策）；基于该框架系统分析已发表系统的能力与局限。

Result: 发现当前方法存在"语法陷阱"——模型倾向于生成语法合理的代码而非物理正确的硬件；工具碎片化与设计上下文丢失使决策对后续阶段的影响被掩盖；现有方法难以扩展到工业级设计规模。

Conclusion: 需转向构建标准化、物理感知的协调器，跨EDA流程连接工具与代理，以实现更可靠、更易用的硬件设计自动化。

Abstract: Electronic design automation (EDA) has advanced engineering productivity through successive generations of tooling that progressively automate synthesis, optimisation, and verification. Large language models (LLMs) extend this trajectory by enabling direct translation from design intent to hardware implementations. In most of the EDA literature, LLM-based solutions are typically assisting siloed design stages or tasks, however this obscured the drivers by which capability emerges and systems scale. In this Perspective, we instead define three hierarchical roles that reveal how capability accumulates: a Generator that produces design artifacts in a single pass, an Agent that refines outputs through iterative tool feedback, and an Orchestrator that coordinates decisions across EDA-stages. Across published systems, this reveals a syntax trap in which models are trained to produce plausible code rather than physically correct hardware, compounded by fragmented tools and loss of design context that obscure how decisions affect later stages. Comparisons across the three roles show that current approaches struggle to scale to industrial designs, motivating a shift towards a standardised, physics-aware orchestrator that connects tools and agents across the EDA flow for more reliable and accessible hardware design.

</details>

### [7] [Zero-Shot Self-Orchestration with Ledger-Based Control for Improved LLM Coding Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26480)
*Victor Gao,Vida Khosrowshahi,Ali Khosrowshahi,Xihao Sun,Juhyun Lee,Simon,Lee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在无额外训练的情况下，引入管理器-工作者框架能显著提升部分大模型（如Qwen3.8-27B、GPT-5.6-Luna等）在LiveCodeBench上的表现（提升8%-42%），且比直接使用更大模型更具成本效益；但该框架对某些模型（如Qwen3.6-35B）效果为负面或无效。主要增益机制包括上下文管理和问题分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于多智能体大语言模型系统优于单模型基线的报告存在证据不一致的问题，且对比实验通常存在混淆因素（如流水线同时改变了token预算、工具调用和提示词），导致难以确定性能提升的真正来源。研究旨在隔离分析引入管理器-工作者架构对共享文件系统工作空间的实际影响。

Method: 在共享文件系统工作空间上引入管理器-工作者框架，不进行额外训练且不针对基准测试进行调优，与同一模型单次回答进行对比实验。测试涵盖9个模型（5个开源模型，参数量从9B到约2.8T；4个前沿闭源模型），在100道最新的LiveCodeBench难题上进行评估，并进行成对实验设计。通过转录分析识别性能增益背后的机制。

Result: 框架效果取决于具体模型：部分模型获得显著提升（Qwen3.8-27B +23.4，GPT-5.6-Luna +10.6，GPT-5.6-Terra +8.0，Kimi-K3 +30.4，Minimax-M3 +11.0，统计显著性p<10^{-4}）；部分模型效果为负面或无效（Qwen3.6-35B -1到-9）。使用管理器后，Opus-5达到研究中的最高分91%。管理器使token成本增加约3倍，但比升级到更大模型更具性价比：GPT-5.6-Terra配合管理器以1/5成本（$11.71 vs $61.11）接近Fable 5单次调用的准确率（85.0% vs 87.4%）。发现两个重复出现的增益机制：上下文管理（通过短工作调用和共享笔记组织状态、减少截断）和问题分解。

Conclusion: 管理器-工作者框架的真实收益存在但具有条件性：对于启用推理的大型模型改进有限，但对禁用推理的某些模型和启用推理的小型模型改进较大。该框架在特定场景下提供了一种比升级模型更具成本效益的性能提升方案，其核心机制在于上下文管理和问题分解能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model systems are widely reported to beat single-model baselines, but the evidence is mixed, and comparisons are usually confounded: pipelines change token budgets, tool calls, and prompts simultaneously, so an aggregate gain rarely reveals what actually helped. We investigate the effect of introducing the manager-worker scaffold over a shared filesystem workspace, with no training and no per-benchmark tuning, measured against the same model answering in a single pass. Across nine models -- five open-weight, spanning 9B to ~2.8T parameters, and four frontier closed models -- on the 100 latest hard LiveCodeBench problems, the scaffold's benefit is real but conditional: large and statistically significant for some (Qwen3.8-27B +23.4, GPT-5.6-Luna +10.6 and GPT-5.6-Terra +8.0, each over five paired passes; Kimi-K3 +30.4 and Minimax-M3 +11.0 over five paired passes with reasoning off, both at $p < 10^{-4}$, and +42 and +12 in a single pass at a 128k cap) and null or negative for others (Qwen3.6-35B -1 to -9 with reasoning off). With the manager, Opus-5 achieves the highest score in the study at 91% in one pass. Running a manager roughly triples the token bill, but it buys accuracy more cheaply than moving to a larger model does: GPT-5.6-Terra with a manager nearly matches Fable 5's single-call accuracy (85.0 against 87.4, $p = 0.59$) at a fifth of the price (\$11.71 against \$61.11 per 100-problem pass, $p < 10^{-4}$), and the Qwen-27B arm does it for \$51.75 on weights anyone can self-host. Our transcript analysis finds several mechanisms behind the gains, of which two recur: context management, in which short worker calls and shared notes organize state and reduce truncation, and problem decomposition. Improvements are modest for large models with reasoning enabled, but larger for some models with reasoning disabled and for smaller models with reasoning enabled.

</details>


### [8] [Risks and Controls for Multi-Agent Systems: an analytical framework for deployment of AI agents across organisational boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26626)
*Alistair Reid,Simon O'Callaghan,Dustin Venini,Liam Carroll,Tiberio Caetano*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本报告提出了一个框架，用于分析AI代理在跨组织边界交互时产生的风险，并提出了三个部署层级（单一治理、联邦治理、开放环境）及其对应的风险因素、失效模式和可用控制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着组织部署AI代理，这些代理将在组织内部、与合作伙伴、客户、供应商的代理以及开放互联网上的未知方进行日益频繁的交互。失效可能从交互本身产生，且当交互跨越组织边界时，没有任何单一组织能够完全观察、控制或治理这些交互。

Method: 引入三个部署层级，由任何两个交互代理之间的最低共同治理定义：单一治理（一个组织治理所有代理）、联邦治理（多个组织在商定规则下部署到共享环境）、开放环境（代理在没有中央权威的情况下运作，共享标准采用是自愿的）。在每个层级内，检查风险因素、失效模式和可用控制措施，识别谁有条件应用这些控制，以及在没有参与者有条件行动时，刻画差距和所需的集体行动。

Result: 报告识别了三个部署层级内的风险因素、失效模式和可用控制措施，并确定了谁有条件应用这些控制；在没有参与者有条件行动的情况下，刻画了差距和解决这些差距所需的集体行动。

Conclusion: 当AI代理交互跨越组织边界时，存在治理真空和需要集体行动才能解决的差距。报告为组织、政策制定者和研究人员提供了推理这些风险以及相应控制措施的框架。

Abstract: This report presents a framework to help organisations, policymakers and researchers reason about the risks that emerge when AI agents interact with each other, how those risks change as interactions cross organisational boundaries, and the controls that may help address them.
  As organisations deploy AI agents, those agents will increasingly interact with each other: inside the organisation, with the agents of partners, customers and suppliers, and with unknown counterparties on the open internet. Failures can emerge from the interactions themselves, and once those interactions cross an organisation's perimeter, no single organisation can fully see, control or govern them.
  The report introduces three deployment tiers, defined by the minimum common governance binding any two interacting agents: singular governance, where one organisation governs every agent; federated governance, where multiple organisations deploy into a shared environment under agreed rules; and open environments, where agents operate with no central authority and shared standards are adopted voluntarily if at all.
  Within each tier, the report examines risk factors, failure modes and available controls. It identifies who is positioned to apply the controls, and where no actor is positioned to act, it characterises the gap and the collective action required to close it.

</details>


### [9] [Fixed-Haven Reservation for Online Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Dense Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26759)
*Taisei Hirayama,Kohei Yoshida,Hiroki Sakaji,Itsuki Noda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出固定安全港(Fixed-Haven)预约机制，解决了单通道、死胡同和树状路径等复杂仓库布局下的在线多智能体取送货问题，在所有测试配置中实现100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集仓库常包含单通道、死胡同和树状路径，缺乏额外等待点，现有MAPD算法依赖的双连通拓扑假设在这些布局中失效，无法保证完成所有任务。

Method: 提出固定安全港契约：每个智能体拥有固定安全港(通常是起始位置)，仅所有者可占用，其他智能体视为障碍。实现SHARP算法，为每个繁忙或撤退中的智能体规划通往其安全港的无碰撞预留路径。

Result: SHARP在所有测试配置中达到100%成功率，是唯一在鲁棒性扫描中完全成功的方法。在树状布局中中央化规划成本较高。禁用中途重新分配导致服务时间恶化1.89倍，完工时间恶化1.53倍。

Conclusion: 固定返回机制是树状布局中保证鲁棒性的核心机制。固定安全港预约方法成功解决了复杂仓库布局下的MAPD问题，虽然规划成本增加但保证了任务完成的可靠性。

Abstract: Dense warehouses often contain single-lane aisles, dead ends, and tree-like guidepaths that leave little room for idle agents to wait without blocking others. Existing Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD) guarantees for completing all finitely released tasks typically rely on extra waiting endpoints that planned paths can avoid, or on biconnected topology; these assumptions may fail in such layouts. We study fixed-Haven reservation for online MAPD, where pickup-delivery tasks are released over time. Each agent owns a fixed Safe Haven (Haven for short), usually its start cell, that only the owner may occupy and that other agents treat as blocked. For finite task releases, we prove that this fixed-Haven contract completes all released tasks under Haven-Reachability and explicit planning/progress assumptions. We implement the contract in SHARP, a Safe-Haven Retreat Planner that keeps every busy or retreating agent on a collision-free reserved route ending at its Haven. We compare SHARP with representative TP and PIBT-family MAPD baselines: Token Passing (TP), Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT), and PIBT with Temporary Priority and Temporary Avoidance (PIBTTP-TA) for biconnected main areas with attached trees. In the robustness sweep, SHARP is the only method with 100% success on all tested configurations, at substantially higher centralized planning cost on tree-like layouts. A TP-style fixed-home-return counterfactual with full-route validation also recovers robustness on tested tree-like layouts, suggesting that fixed return is a central robustness mechanism there. A no-overwrite variant shows that disabling mid-retreat reassignment worsens service time (release-to-delivery latency) by 1.89 times and makespan by 1.53 times in the tested high-load tree condition.

</details>


### [10] [Dynamic Haven Selection for Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Constrained Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26939)
*Taisei Hirayama,Kohei Yoshida,Hiroki Sakaji,Itsuki Noda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: A-sharp通过自适应更换避难点（Haven）目标，在保持安全性的前提下显著降低了多智能体拣选配送任务的最大完成时间，中位数降低达16.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHARP算法要求智能体在完成任务后返回初始指定的避难点，当配送点距离避难点较远时会造成不必要的行程浪费。作者希望通过自适应更换避难点来提高效率。

Method: A-sharp在任务分配时动态更换智能体的避难点目标，通过可用性测试确保候选避难点可用，并使用待释放规则保护原避难点直到智能体离开，从而避免冲突。

Result: 在72,000次运行测试中，A-sharp与SHARP均完成所有任务；在138个避难点数量超过智能体数量的配置中，A-sharp在107个配置上显著优于SHARP（经Holm校正的成对比较），在测试的树形地图上中位数降低16.7%的最大完成时间。

Conclusion: A-sharp成功实现了避难点的自适应分配，在保持安全性（不变性保持和有限释放完整性）的同时显著提升了系统效率，为空间受限仓库布局中的多智能体配送问题提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Space-efficient warehouse layouts often contain single-agent-width aisles and dead-end workstations where robots have few places to wait without blocking others. In Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD) on such constrained layouts, robots must accept online pickup-delivery tasks while preserving protected waiting locations called Havens. The Safe HAven Retreat Planner (SHARP) introduced a mechanism that extends each committed task path with a validated retreat to the agent's dedicated initial Haven, but fixed-Haven commitments can send agents toward distant Havens after deliveries. We present A-sharp (Adaptive SHARP), which changes an agent's retreat target at task assignment time. A naive switch can cause two agents to rely on the same waiting location or let another committed path pass through a location that is still occupied or reserved. A-sharp prevents these failures with an availability test for candidate Havens and a pending-release rule that keeps the previous Haven protected until the agent departs. Under explicit Haven-structure and Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP) assumptions, we prove invariant preservation and finite-release completeness: every task in any finite release sequence is delivered in finite time. Across 72,000 runs on 14,400 paired map-agent-count-rate-seed cases over four maps, both SHARP and A-sharp complete their respective 14,400 runs. For makespan (final delivery time), a prespecified paired comparison with Holm correction over all 138 configurations with more Havens than agents finds A-sharp significantly better in 107 configurations and never significantly worse than SHARP; on the tested tree map, the median reduction is 16.7%.

</details>


### [11] [One Model, Many Minds: Unlocking Multi-Agent Synergy in a Single Agent via Mixture of Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27338)
*Zhichen Zeng,Huiyuan Chen,Jingru Cheng,Juan Zha,Ming Liu,Ying Chen,Xiyuan Yang,Chaosheng Dong,Haiyang Zhang,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出MoRe框架，通过动态融合多个角色引导向量，在单智能体单轮推理中实现多视角专业化，性能媲美多智能体系统且大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体方法无法适应多样化查询，而多智能体系统虽能实现动态多视角问题求解，但需要多轮交互导致上下文过长和推理成本高昂。

Method: MoRe学习一个包含多样化角色引导向量的码本，通过查询感知路由器动态融合这些向量生成复合引导向量，冻结骨干LLM并通过三阶段SFT课程和GRPO后训练进行训练。

Result: 在推理和个性基准测试中，MoRe平均比单智能体基线提升2.2%，性能与多智能体系统相当，同时减少20倍的token成本。

Conclusion: MoRe成功将多视角专业化能力融入单智能体单轮推理过程，有效平衡了专业化性能和计算效率，为LLM专业化提供了一种新的解决方案。

Abstract: Specializing Large Language Models (LLMs) toward distinct abilities underpins successes ranging from personalized assistants to multi-agent systems (MAS). Single-agent paradigms rely on pre-defined personas or steering vectors to induce specialization, yet they impose a single fixed specialization that fails to adapt to diverse queries. Conversely, MAS achieves dynamic multi-perspective problem solving by orchestrating agents with distinct text-based roles, but fusing these specializations requires multi-turn interactions that inflate context length and inference cost. To address these limitations, we propose Mixture of Roles (MoRe), which adaptively composes multiple specializations into a single steering vector for single-turn inference. Specifically, MoRe learns a diversified codeboox of steering vectors, each of which encodes a latent role. A query-aware router dynamically fuses the codebook into a steering vector that encompasses multiple roles. By steering the backbone LLM with the composed vector, MoRe enables multi-perspective specialization in a single-agent, single-turn inference process. The proposed MoRe can be efficiently trained via a three-stage SFT curriculum and GRPO post-training, while the backbone LLM remains frozen. Experiments across reasoning and personality benchmarks show that MoRe outperforms single-agent baselines by 2.2% on average, and achieves performance on par with MAS while reducing token cost by 20x.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [12] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EduRiskX框架，一个结合时序Transformer和F-Logic符号推理的神经符号系统，用于在线教育中学生学业风险的早期预测与解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学生学业风险预测模型存在早期检测能力有限和可解释性不足的问题，导致"黑箱"信任危机，阻碍了其在实际教学场景中的应用。

Method: 提出EduRiskX神经符号框架：(1)神经组件：基于时序Transformer，采用时间注意力、类别加权损失和动态周截断建模学生纵向活动序列；(2)符号组件：基于参与理论和学生整合模型构建F-Logic规则库，模拟教育者的诊断逻辑；(3)融合机制：通过逻辑回归学习神经风险概率和符号置信度分数的相对贡献。

Result: 在OULAD数据集上（80/10/10学生级别划分），学期末（第38周）准确率达0.900，F1分数0.894；平均早期检测周为9.32，检测率94.30%；在相同条件下相比PatchTST、iTransformer、LSTM、CNN等基线模型，召回率和早期识别能力均有提升。

Conclusion: EduRiskX不仅提升了预测性能，还通过F-Logic模块提供了结构化的、基于规则的解释，将预测与可观察的行为模式和教育理论联系起来，解决了可解释性问题。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [13] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了独立LLM与四步代理流程在ICU死亡率预测解释中的表现，发现代理流程在指南依据和细节特异性上更优，而独立LLM在SHAP特征对齐和方向一致性上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型虽能准确预测ICU死亡率，但单纯的特征归因方法难以提供临床所需的解释性叙述。研究旨在探索LLM及其代理流程能否弥合这一差距，为临床床边决策提供可解释性支持。

Method: 使用eICU Demo数据集（2,353例ICU住院病例，死亡率8.1%）训练XGBoost模型（AUROC 0.855，AUPRC 0.332）。对38例分层解释子集，对比独立LLM与四步代理流程（分离数据解释、指南检查和最终解释）的表现，评估包括结果泄露、SHAP对齐、方向一致性、指南依据、数值特异性和合理性。

Result: 独立LLM产生1例显性结果泄露，代理流程无泄露。在14例SHAP审查重叠病例中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐度（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）更高；代理流程在指南依据（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和合理性（0.700 vs 0.671）上更优。

Conclusion: 代理分解能提升安全相关的指南依据和患者特异性细节，但在高风险风险解释应用前，应与基于归因的检查相结合，以确保临床安全性和解释质量。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [14] [Agent Mesh: Reliability Primitives for Non-Idempotent Agent Delegation - Identity Adequacy and Evidence Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26225)
*Mazhar Shaikh,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde,Harshal Pathak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 生产环境自主智能体平台的故障研究表明，服务网格的重试、超时和熔断机制在智能体场景中失效，根本原因是身份充分性和证据充分性不足，作者提出了7个新的可靠性原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在编排器管理下执行有界软件任务，编排器采用服务网格的重试、超时和熔断机制。然而这些机制在智能体场景中的有效性未经充分验证，需要通过实际故障研究来评估其适用性。

Method: 对生产环境智能体软件交付平台进行故障研究，涵盖147个编号事件，跨81次运行，每次运行都有成本度量，大多数案例有可复现故障的变异证明。通过定量分析验证传统可靠性原语的三个假设是否在实践中被违反。

Result: 发现三个假设均被违反：（1）连续54次工具调用成功但无熔断机制可检测；（2）进度信号恒定导致第三轮修复误触发；（3）跨6次调用的21个事件使幂等组件无法成功。发现两类跨领域根因：身份充分性（5个子系统因身份辨识不足产生错误答案）和证据充分性（可靠性决策需基于可归因、确定性的证据）。

Conclusion: 传统服务网格原语不适合智能体系统，提出7个新的可靠性原语，其执行单元是委托而非消息，并规划了受控评估实验以进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly perform bounded software tasks under an orchestrator that retries, resumes, and budgets them. The machinery such orchestrators reach for is the service mesh's: retry, timeout, and error-rate circuit breaking. We report a failure study of a production agentic software-delivery platform over 147 numbered incidents spanning 81 runs, each with a measured cost and, in most cases, a mutation proof reproducing the failure. All three assumptions those primitives rest on are violated in practice, and we quantify the consequences: a loop of fifty-four consecutive successful tool calls no error-rate breaker could see; a progress signal constant by construction, guaranteeing a false trip on the third repair round and driving one run from six of six components to three; twenty-one events accumulated across six invocations of one delegation, making a correct, idempotent component unwinnable; a misrouted failure that woke five components for a two-component fault, leaving three bystanders regressing working code; and twelve incidents in which the enforcement layer blocked correct work, the most expensive costing 107 agent turns and zero accepted writes. We find one cross-cutting cause and its dual. Identity adequacy: in five separate subsystems an identity that failed to discriminate produced a confident wrong answer, and two of them derived the corrective rule independently. Evidence adequacy: a reliability decision may be taken only on evidence capable of moving, attributable to what it measures, and deterministic under identical conditions. From the findings we derive seven reliability primitives whose enforcement unit is the delegation rather than the message, and specify the controlled evaluation the study motivates but does not constitute.

</details>


### [15] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练的大模型在电池预测与健康管理(BPHM)领域的应用，提出从数据稀缺缓解、泛化能力提升、领域知识融合和系统级自动化四个维度突破传统方法瓶颈的研究框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法(物理模型和任务导向深度学习)在计算效率、跨域泛化、标注数据依赖和可解释性方面存在长期瓶颈。基于Transformer和自监督预训练的大模型为解决这些挑战提供了变革性新范式，亟需系统梳理其应用现状和发展路径。

Method: 系统阐述大模型基础技术(Transformer架构、自监督学习、多模态数据集、PEFT技术)，并从四个关键维度分类综述最新进展：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、融合领域知识提升可解释性、实现系统级自动化。

Result: 识别了当前挑战：数据可获取性、智能验证、可信度和部署可行性。提出了未来研究路线图：构建协作数据生态系统、验证工业应用智能、通过物理信息设计增强可信度、实现高效端侧部署。

Conclusion: 建立了理解和推进大模型驱动BPHM的系统方法，为研究人员和从业者开发下一代电池管理系统提供了关键洞察，助力实现电池全生命周期的安全、可靠和自主运行。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [16] [SKILL.state: Scalable Long-Horizon Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26263)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari,Jonghyun Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SKILL.state运行时架构，通过用显式可变执行状态替代传统仅追加的对话历史，解决了大语言模型代理在执行长流程任务时的延迟增加和上下文污染问题，在提高任务准确率的同时大幅降低了令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代理运行时采用持续追加观察、动作和中间推理痕迹到对话历史的方式，导致长时序任务执行中出现延迟退化和上下文污染失败问题，缺乏有效的长期执行状态管理机制。

Method: 提出SKILL.state运行时架构，在每个执行步骤仅向模型提供不可变的技能规范、当前结构化执行状态和最新观察，中间推理在生成验证后的状态更新后立即丢弃，防止执行历史导致的提示词增长，从而替代传统的仅追加对话历史方式。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型和执行环境上的实验表明，SKILL.state在提升任务准确率的同时大幅降低了累积令牌消耗，验证了显式执行状态作为可扩展长时序代理技能抽象的有效性和架构无关性。

Conclusion: 显式执行状态是一种有效且架构无关的抽象机制，能够支持可扩展的长时序代理技能执行，为大语言模型代理的长程任务执行提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents executing complex, long-running procedural skills. Existing agent runtimes maintain execution by continually appending observations, actions, and intermediate reasoning traces to an ever-growing conversation history, causing latency degradation and context-poisoning failures over long horizons. We present SKILL.state, a runtime architecture that replaces append-only conversational history with an explicit, mutable execution state. At each execution step, the model receives only the immutable skill specification, the current structured execution state, and the latest observation. Intermediate reasoning is discarded immediately after producing a validated state update, preventing prompt growth with execution history. Across diverse datasets, models, and execution environments, SKILL.state improves task accuracy while substantially reducing cumulative token consumption. Our results demonstrate that explicit execution state is an effective and architecture-agnostic abstraction for scalable long-horizon agent skills.

</details>


### [17] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PICasso框架，实现了从自然语言到光子集成电路（PIC）的自动化设计流程，通过结构化生成、物理验证和仿真优化，显著提升了设计成功率和性能，并引入PIC-Set基准系统评估了多种大语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PIC设计依赖手动GUI工作流程，耗时且易出错。现有大语言模型生成光子电路设计时缺乏领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈，导致生成结果不可靠且难以制造。需要一种自动化框架将LLM转化为实用的PIC设计代理。

Method: PICasso采用多阶段流水线：1) 自然语言到YAML到GDS的结构化生成管道；2) PDK知识注入；3) 自动布局布线；4) DRC/LVS物理验证；5) 基于SAX的光子仿真与优化。同时引入PIC-Set基准数据集（36个参数化PIC设计任务），提出结构/功能Spec@k、优化效率和扰动鲁棒性等新评价指标。

Result: 在PIC-Set基准测试中，PICasso相比原生LLM生成显著提升性能：结构Spec@3达92.7%，功能Spec@3达52%（高复杂度电路）；通过仿真引导优化将平均插入损耗从4.98 dB降至3.25 dB（改善1.74 dB）；运行时间与手动GUI流程相当。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈机制能够有效转化LLM为实用的PIC设计代理，使其从脆弱的网表生成器转变为能够生成可制造布局的工具，为光子集成电路自动化设计开辟新途径。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [18] [SIGMA: Structured Noise-Effect-Aware Grouped Multi-Agent Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26683)
*Li Mingqian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了多智能体强化学习中噪声观测会产生结构化噪声效应，提出了SIGMA分层协作框架，通过密度分组、组内共识聚合和组间注意力机制来学习鲁棒表征，在星际争霸II任务中验证了该方法能有效提升噪声环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒多智能体强化学习方法忽视了观测噪声在合作决策中产生的下游结构化影响。虽然各智能体的观测扰动独立引入，但通过底层的合作结构，这些扰动对决策的影响会在任务相关性强的智能体之间呈现局部相关性，而在全局上呈现异质性。现有方法未能显式刻画或利用这种结构依赖的噪声效应。

Method: 提出SIGMA分层协作框架，包含三个关键步骤：(1) 基于密度的分组策略自适应组织智能体形成局部结构；(2) 组内共识聚合机制保留共享的任务相关信息并平滑智能体特定的表征偏差；(3) 组间注意力机制自适应整合不同组的信息，在保持全局协调的同时容纳各组异质性贡献。

Result: 在星际争霸II的噪声观测任务上进行实验验证：(1) 经验性地验证了结构化噪声效应的存在；(2) SIGMA在存在观测噪声的情况下持续提升鲁棒性；(3) 在无噪声环境中保持竞争力性能。

Conclusion: 论文成功刻画了多智能体系统中结构化噪声效应的现象，并证明了通过显式利用合作结构可以有效提升系统在噪声观测下的鲁棒性，为鲁棒多智能体协作提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces significant challenges in maintaining robust coordination under noisy observations. Although observation disturbances are often introduced independently across agents, their downstream effects on cooperative decision-making can become structured through underlying cooperation structures. We characterize this phenomenon as structured noise effects, where noise-induced decision effects exhibit local correlation among agents with stronger task-related dependencies while remaining globally heterogeneous across different agents and local structures. Existing robust MARL methods, however, rarely explicitly characterize or exploit such structure-dependent noise effects. To address this limitation, we propose SIGMA, a hierarchical collaboration framework that exploits cooperation structures to learn robust representations under noisy observations. SIGMA first organizes agents into adaptive local structures through density-based grouping and performs intra-group consensus aggregation to preserve shared task-relevant information while smoothing agent-specific representation deviations. Inter-group attention then adaptively integrates information across different groups to preserve global coordination while accommodating their heterogeneous contributions. Experiments on noisy-observation tasks in StarCraft II empirically validate the structured noise effects and demonstrate that SIGMA consistently improves robustness under observation noise while maintaining competitive performance in noise-free environments.

</details>


### [19] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIFQA是一个基于多智能体大语言模型（LLM）的确定性工具驱动框架，专用于计算密集型金融问答。在定期存款问答基准测试中，CIFQA在计算密集型查询上实现95.54%准确率，总体准确率达90.87%，显著优于直接使用LLM的基线方法，且17B开源模型在CIFQA框架下优于更大的前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）在自然语言任务中表现优异，但在处理多步骤金融计算时往往产生数值错误但看似合理的答案。计算密集型金融问答需要精确推理结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和规则约束，而现有LLM难以满足这些精确计算要求。

Method: CIFQA框架将语言理解与数值执行分离：采用专门的智能体负责查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，同时使用基于Python的确定性工具执行金融计算和规则应用。在定期存款问答场景中实例化该框架进行验证。

Result: CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%准确率，总体准确率90.87%，显著优于提供完整公式、利率表和基准指令的直接LLM基线。消融实验表明精确利率查找、期限计算、滚动年度调整和提前取款逻辑等确定性组件是性能的关键贡献者。17B开源骨干模型在CIFQA架构下优于更大规模的前沿模型。

Conclusion: 架构设计比模型规模对数值可靠性更为重要。CIFQA为计算密集型金融推理任务提供了一个可泛化的框架，证明了将语言理解与确定性计算分离的有效性，为金融领域的精确问答提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [20] [Decoupling Planning and Control for Instructable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26788)
*Zineng Tang,Kelsey R. Allen,Sjoerd van Steenkiste,Ishita Dasgupta,Alane Suhr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Instruct-to-Act框架，将视觉-语言模型的高层规划能力与世界模型控制器的快速执行能力结合，通过合成指令重新标注和联合优化，使控制器能够理解语言指令并高频自主执行，在7个具身环境中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，预训练指令微调的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在将指令和观察映射到高层计划方面表现出色，但在陌生环境中难以将这些计划实现为可靠且低延迟的动作序列。同时，世界模型控制器擅长快速的观察到动作控制，但缺乏开放式的任务指导。这种互补的能力缺口促使研究者探索如何将两者的优势整合到一个统一系统中。

Method: 提出Instruct-to-Act框架，训练世界模型控制器在高频下自主行动，条件是VLM规划师生成的稀疏、高延迟和高层文本指令。为使控制器具备语言指令能力，使用合成指令重新标注控制器策略轨迹片段，并联合优化行为克隆目标与现有的奖励最大化和世界建模目标。这种解耦设计允许在匹配的观察和动作空间下，直接替换不同的预训练VLM规划器而无需微调。

Result: 在7个具身环境（包括3个多智能体环境）中进行评估，在该框架下，VLM规划器通过语言协调，训练后的控制器作为其执行器。在匹配的观察和动作空间下，该解耦方法持续优于仅控制器和直接VLM动作生成变体，保持快速控制特性，支持无需微调即可替换不同预训练VLM规划器，在7个任务中的6个上与强视觉-语言-动作和多智能体强化学习基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Instruct-to-Act成功实现了VLM高层规划与世界模型控制器快速执行的有机结合，通过语言条件化实现了两者的有效协同。该方法不仅克服了单一系统的局限性，还提供了灵活的模块化架构，在多智能体协调和快速控制等实际应用场景中展现出良好潜力，为构建更强大、更通用的具身智能系统提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pre-trained, instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) perform well at mapping from instructions and observations to high-level plans, but struggle to realize such plans as reliable low-latency action sequences in unfamiliar environments. At the same time, world-model controllers excel at fast observation-to-action control, but lack open-ended task guidance. In this work, we combine these strengths into a single system, Instruct-to-Act, where we train a world-model controller to act autonomously at high frequency when conditioned on sparse, higher-latency, and high-level text instructions generated by a VLM planner. To train controllers to be language-instructable, we relabel segments of controller policy rollouts with synthetic instructions and jointly optimize a behavior-cloning objective along with existing reward-maximizing and world-modeling objectives. We evaluate our proposed approach across seven embodied environments, including three multi-agent environments where VLM planners coordinate through language while trained controllers serve as their actuators. Under matched observation and action spaces, our decoupled approach consistently outperforms controller-only and direct VLM action-generation variants, preserves fast control, and lets us swap in different pretrained VLM planners without fine-tuning, while remaining competitive with strong vision-language-action and multi-agent RL baselines on six of seven tasks.

</details>


### [21] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自主目标强化学习的闭环框架CARL，用于探索和控制复杂系统（以连续细胞自动机Lenia为例）。该智能体能自主发现稳定模式、控制模式运动，并实现人机协作，在多种分布外条件下展现出强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的复杂系统探索方法大多采用开环模式，即设定初始条件后执行完整模拟并观察结果，过程中无法干预。这种被动观测的方式限制了对复杂系统中涌现现象的主动探索和控制能力。为了突破这一局限，研究者希望引入一种闭环框架，使智能体能够在系统运行过程中通过局部干预主动引导复杂系统的演化。

Method: 提出了一种基于自目的性强化学习（autotelic reinforcement learning）的闭环框架。在该框架中，智能体自主采样多样化的目标，并学习一个目标条件策略（goal-conditioned policy），通过最小化的局部扰动干预复杂系统。该框架被实例化为CARL系统，应用于连续细胞自动机Lenia。

Result: 在Lenia上展示了三项核心能力：(1) 相比启发式基线方法，CARL在更广泛的Lenia更新规则上以更高的成功率发现稳定的孤子（solitons）；(2) 能够通过少量干预操控现有孤子的移动方向，证明不仅能创造自组织模式，还能控制它们；(3) 人类可以通过提供高级方向指令实时引导孤子穿越迷宫环境，智能体将其转化为低级干预。经过多样化目标、更新规则和随机初始状态训练的智能体，能够在各种分布外条件下实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 本研究开辟了一条通向人工实验智能体的路径，这类智能体能够自主地或在人类指导下，发现并控制复杂系统中的涌现现象。该闭环框架为复杂系统的主动探索和控制提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [22] [LiveSim: Simulating Environment-Shaped Users in Multi-Agent Live-Stream Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26849)
*Jiaqi Xu,Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LiveSim框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）模拟直播生态系统中的用户行为，通过可编辑的行为假设和基于轨迹的交互逐步优化用户画像，从而提升个体行为保真度并支持生态系统层面的仿真分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户行为模拟器通常依赖从历史观测中推断出的静态用户画像，这在直播等社交密集型环境中表现不足，因为交互动态会持续重塑用户行为，静态画像无法捕捉这种环境塑造效应。

Method: 提出LiveSim框架，将用户表示为可编辑的行为假设，通过基于轨迹的交互逐步优化：模拟轨迹与观测轨迹之间的差异揭示缺失的环境塑造效应，将这些信号提取为可迁移的环境-行为模式，并累积到集体行为记忆中，以提升用户级行为保真度并支持生态系统级仿真。

Result: 在真实直播风控数据上的实验验证了LiveSim在提升用户级行为保真度方面的有效性，并能够支持生态系统层面的风险演化和平台干预效果分析。

Conclusion: LiveSim通过动态行为假设更新和集体行为记忆机制，有效解决了社交密集型环境中用户行为模拟的挑战，为直播生态系统仿真提供了可行的解决方案，具有重要的实践应用价值。

Abstract: User behavior simulation with large language models~(LLMs) is increasingly used to support multi-agent ecosystem simulation. Existing simulators typically rely on static user profiles inferred from historical observations, which become inadequate in socially intensive environments such as live streaming where interaction dynamics continuously reshape user behavior. We propose \textbf{LiveSim}, an LLM-based framework for live-stream ecosystem simulation. It represents users as editable behavioral hypotheses and progressively refines them through trajectory-grounded interactions, where discrepancies between simulated and observed trajectories reveal missing environmental shaping effects. These signals are further extracted as transferable environment-behavior patterns and accumulated in a collective behavioral memory to improve user-level behavioral fidelity and support ecosystem-level simulation. Experiments on real-world live-stream risk-control data validate the effectiveness of LiveSim in improving user-level behavioral fidelity and enabling ecosystem-level analysis of risk evolution and platform intervention effects.

</details>


### [23] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了能源预测中的"精度-效率悖论"：高精度模型反而可能导致净能源亏损。作者提出总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一建模为能源损失，在热敏感边缘环境中证明复杂架构的高运行强度往往抵消了其精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源预测追求高精度以提升能效并减少浪费，但在边缘计算等关键任务场景中，高精度模型可能因推理能耗和电池老化而适得其反。作者旨在解决这一悖论，确保预测模型在实际部署中真正实现能源节约而非净亏损。

Method: 提出面向能源预测的总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一建模为能源损失（电池老化被视为系统能源承载能力的物理耗散），以最小化净能源损失为目标进行框架设计与分析。

Result: 在热敏感边缘环境中证明，复杂高精度架构通过高精度节省的能源往往被其高运行强度带来的总能源损失（推理能耗与电池老化）所抵消，验证了精度-效率悖论的存在。

Conclusion: 高精度能源预测模型在边缘环境中不一定带来净能源收益，需采用TCO框架统筹考虑推理能耗与电池老化，以实现真正的能源效率最大化。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>


### [24] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力市场中自主学习算法可能导致默契合谋的风险，通过多智能体强化学习建模和实验验证了这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场参与者越来越多地采用基于学习的代理进行投标策略，使市场变得算法化。其他领域的算法市场证据表明，独立学习可能导致默契合谋。电力市场通常具有寡头垄断特征，少数参与者之间重复互动，结构上容易产生非竞争行为。

Method: 将策略性投标建模为具有不完全公共监测的重复博弈，使用多智能体强化学习建模参与者的涌现行为。提出一套超越纳什均衡利润比较的多维标准来评估结果行为是否构成默契合谋。

Result: 实验结果表明这种风险在电力市场中是现实存在的：在某些情况下，代理确实学会了维持超竞争性结果并支持默契合谋指标，尽管代理从未被指示进行合谋。

Conclusion: 电力市场中自主学习算法的出现确实存在默契合谋的真实危险，这对市场监管者和政策制定者具有重要的启示意义。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior. In the face of these observations, this paper investigates the hypothesis that tacit collusion may emerge in electricity markets where participants' actions are controlled by autonomous learning-based algorithms. We model strategic bidding as a repeated game with imperfect public monitoring, and model the participants' emergent behavior using multi-agent reinforcement learning. We propose a multi-dimensional set of criteria (going beyond profit comparisons against Nash equilibria) to assess whether the resulting behavior constitutes tacit collusion. Our experimental results showcase that such a danger is realistic for electricity markets: there are cases where agents do learn to sustain supra-competitive outcomes that are supportive of tacit collusion indicators, even though the agents were never instructed to collude.

</details>


### [25] [LLMs for Academic Workflows: An Evaluation of Literature Reviews Generated with Short and Long Context Windows of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26145)
*Muhammad Ali Chaudhry,Xinyuan Hao,Haifa Alwahaby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型在短上下文和长上下文设置下生成的文献综述质量，发现随着上下文窗口增大，LLM能够整合更广泛信息并保持连贯性，但会加剧内容重复、遗漏关键工作和描述性倾向等问题，需要人类监督和领域专家改进才能达到学术发表标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究上下文窗口对AI生成文献综述质量的影响，以及AI在支持文献综述写作中的作用，为学术界使用LLM辅助文献综述提供实证依据。

Method: 基于Semantic Scholar和Arxiv的研究资料，生成20篇AI文献综述，由两位研究人员从15个维度对综述进行评估，对比短上下文和长上下文设置下的输出质量差异。

Result: AI生成的文献综述需要人类监督才能达到学术发表标准；随着上下文窗口增加，LLM能够整合更广泛信息并保持长文本连贯性，但同时加剧了内容重复、遗漏关键工作以及偏向描述而非综合分析等问题；AI综述可提供基础概览，但需要领域专家批判性评估和改进。

Conclusion: AI生成的文献综述可作为基础概览工具，但其输出必须经过领域专家的批判性评估和完善；未来研究应考虑整合其他LLM和微调模型，在不同领域采用结合人类专业知识与AI能力的混合方法，以解决本研究识别出的局限性。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [26] [DSA: Evidence-Aware LLM-Agent Orchestration for Multi-Market Stock Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26990)
*Linsen Zhu,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DSA框架，一个用于多市场股票研究的LLM智能体编排系统，通过证据获取、结构化上下文构建、模型路由分析等流程，实现对异构证据的组装和报告生成的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型能够总结金融信息，但一个可操作的股票研究系统必须能够组装异构证据、揭示不可用数据和模型能力，并控制生成意见如何影响最终报告。现有系统缺乏对这些关键环节的系统性处理。

Method: DSA框架将工作流组织为证据获取、结构化上下文构建、模型路由分析、可选角色与策略技能推理、以及报告生成五个阶段。系统包含默认报告配置和智能体配置两种模式，共享证据和模型路由服务但使用不同的输出验证和风险保障机制。在智能体配置中，策略技能意见需经过信号资格分区，显式提供分歧信息给决策智能体，并实施保守风险覆盖。

Result: 参考实现包含六个区域市场路径、十五个内置策略技能、托管和本地模型路由以及多种执行和交付界面。在冻结软件快照时，选定的1,457个可移植离线后端合约测试全部通过；596个测试用例被回顾性映射到六个与LLM智能体架构相关的合约家族。

Conclusion: 这些证据建立了测试软件合约的实现一致性，但并不证明报告质量、预测准确性或投资回报的优越性。该框架成功实现了对股票研究工作流的系统性编排和风险控制。

Abstract: Large language models can summarize financial information, but an operational stock-research system must first assemble heterogeneous evidence, expose unavailable data and model capabilities, and control how generated opinions affect a final report. We present DSA, an evidence-aware orchestration framework for multi-market stock research with large language model (LLM) agents. DSA organizes the workflow into evidence acquisition, structured context construction, model-routed analysis, optional role and Strategy Skill reasoning, and report generation with selected context and diagnostics. A default report profile and an optional agentic profile share evidence and model-routing services but use profile-specific output validation and risk safeguards. In the agentic profile, core role outputs are processed by role-specific parsers, whereas Strategy Skill opinions undergo an additional signal-eligibility partition before synthesis; disagreement is supplied explicitly to the decision agent, followed by a conservative risk override. The reference implementation includes six regional market paths, fifteen bundled Strategy Skills, hosted and local model routes, and multiple execution and delivery surfaces. At a frozen software snapshot, a selected manifest of 1,457 portable offline backend contract tests passed; 596 cases were retrospectively mapped to six contract families central to the reported LLM-agent architecture. This evidence establishes implementation conformance for the tested software contracts, not superior report quality, forecasting accuracy, or investment returns.

</details>


### [27] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系超图变换器（RHT），一种用于多表关系数据学习的统一架构，通过将关系数据库表示为超图并学习五维嵌入（PentE），在医疗健康等领域的关系数据建模中实现了更好的语义连贯性和计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习在医疗健康等复杂信息系统中面临重大挑战。关系数据包含多种复杂性来源：大数据量、高维变量、高基数分类特征、复杂的表间依赖关系以及重复的时间观测。现有的表格、关系和时序图方法在处理这些复杂性时存在局限性，需要一种能够统一处理这些复杂性的架构。

Method: 引入关系超图变换器（RHT）统一架构：1）将关系数据库表示为超图；2）学习五维嵌入（PentE）；3）执行稀疏关系注意力机制，其复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量的平方。在公开的Synthea合成电子健康记录数据集上进行评估，使用每次就诊的SNOMED CT病况代码多标签预测任务。

Result: 在基准测试中，XGBoost实现了最高的罕见代码召回率，而RHT获得了最强的嵌入语义连贯性。RHT在保持计算可扩展性的同时产生了更具语义连贯性的嵌入。消融研究量化了每个架构组件的贡献。与表格、关系和时序图基线相比，RHT展现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: RHT架构成功解决了多表关系数据学习的复杂性挑战，在语义连贯性和计算可扩展性之间取得了良好平衡。该研究提供了开源参考实现和实验协议，计划在获得PhysioNet凭证后对MIMIC-IV进行临床验证。RHT为医疗健康等领域的关系数据建模提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [28] [A Contract-Centered Architecture for Scalable and Manageable Agentic Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27086)
*Yaxiao Liu,Pengbo Liu,Yiwen Liu,Yihua Guan,Zhenghe Hou,Jiaxing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向企业AI部署的可验证运行时架构框架，通过四个责任对象（Skill、Harness、Scaffold和数据基板）解决跨组织协调问题，核心贡献是一个可证伪的假设P1（成本感知的能力-容量可分离性）以及相应的测量协议，但目前尚未有实现或实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署涉及多个业务单元、应用团队、测试、平台工程、基础设施、安全、运营和数据治理等多个部门的协调问题。现有的用例基准测试仅能评估单个智能体完成单个任务的能力，无法评估能力、模型、运行时机制、容量和企业数据如何协同地进行所有权归属、变更、准入和验证。缺乏一种能够测量和管理这些跨组织协调问题的框架。

Method: 提出四个责任对象作为共享组织契约：Skill（可复用、版本化的能力和工作流资产）、Harness（运行时编译器和控制器）、Scaffold（执行/控制边界和非功能性需求所有者）、以及独立CIO治理的数据基板。运行时核心为A = <S, H, X>。提出核心假设P1（成本-aware能力-容量可分离性）：在声明的运行区域内，改变激活的能力可在预注册的等价边界内保持容量-响应交互，改变兼容的Scaffold容量可在非劣性边界内保持能力语义，且所需控制保持在声明的执行预算内。六个设计条件转化为可测量的义务。提出集群周期随机交叉实验设计（平衡顺序、重置/清洗期、重复种子和失败机制、集群感知不确定性），包含四种判定状态：支持、证伪、有条件工程、或不确定。

Result: 本文提出了一个契约边界的运行时架构、源数据保护的数据基板、以及可证伪的测量协议。但本文目前没有报告任何已完成的实现、实验、数据集或测量结果。

Conclusion: 本文为解决企业AI部署的跨组织协调问题提供了理论框架和方法论贡献，包括运行时架构、数据治理机制和可验证的测量协议。该框架的核心创新在于将组织契约与技术架构相结合，并提供可证伪的假设验证方法。然而，该工作目前仍处于理论提案阶段，需要后续的实际实现和实验验证来证明其有效性。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployment is a coordination problem across business units, application and AI teams, testing, platform engineering, infrastructure, security, operations, and data governance. Use-case benchmarks show whether one agent completes one task, but not how changing capabilities, models, runtime mechanisms, capacity, and enterprise data should be owned, changed, admitted, or evidenced together.
  We present four responsibility objects as shared organizational contracts: Skill (reusable, versioned capability and workflow asset), Harness (runtime compiler and governor), Scaffold (execution/control boundary and NFR owner), and a stack-external data substrate under independent CIO-governed semantics and telemetry. The runtime core is A = <S, H, X>, with the data substrate outside that stack.
  The central contribution is one bounded, falsifiable hypothesis, P1 (cost-aware capability-capacity separability): within a declared operating region, changing activated capability preserves the capacity-response interaction within a preregistered equivalence margin, while changing compatible Scaffold capacity preserves capability semantics up to a non-inferiority margin, and the required controls stay within a declared enforcement budget. Six design conditions become measured obligations whose coverage, violations, uncertainty, cost, and exclusions determine whether P1 is decidable.
  We propose a cluster-period randomized crossover experiment (balanced order, reset/washout, repeated seeds and failure regimes, cluster-aware uncertainty) with a four-state verdict: supported, falsified, conditional-engineering, or inconclusive. This paper contributes a contract-bounded runtime architecture, a source-preserving data substrate, and a falsifiable measurement protocol. It reports no completed implementation, experiment, dataset, or measured result.

</details>


### [29] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了LLM管道用于从536篇基于主体的建模论文中提取信息，GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0的论文级准确率分别为77.95%和81.67%，发现LLM间一致性可作为输出质量的指标，揭示了LLM在系统文献综述中的潜力与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的最新进展为简化和自动化系统文献综述(SLRs)等研究流程创造了新机会，本文旨在探索LLM在建模与仿真领域进行大规模SLRs的可行性与准确性。

Method: 开发LLM管道，从536篇同行评审的基于主体建模论文中提取模型相关信息，并将LLM提取结果与人工进行的系统文献综述结果进行对比分析。

Result: 论文级准确率：GPT-4.1约77.95%，GPT-5.0约81.67%；字段级准确率范围32.40%-100.00%，复杂或主观字段可靠性较低。发现LLM间一致性是输出质量的潜在指标：低一致性可能表明幻觉，高一致性但低准确率可能指向人工数据集中的噪声或错误。

Conclusion: 研究为提示词开发提供了实践见解，强调了LLM用于建模与仿真领域全规模系统文献综述的潜力与局限性，为未来LLM辅助文献综述研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [30] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将高性能预测模型与CRM系统集成的方法，通过可解释AI技术（SHAP和LIME）解决了模型不透明问题，预计可降低客户流失率3.3-5.3个百分点，每周期节省19.9万-31.9万美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信行业面临严重的客户流失问题，成熟市场月流失率约1.9%，年损失数十亿美元。虽然预测模型能准确识别高风险客户，但因集成和非线性模型的不透明性，一线CRM工作流程无法有效利用这些模型——流失专员仅凭概率分数无法设计个性化干预措施，缺乏对客户流失原因的理解。

Method: 在IBM电信客户流失基准数据集（7,043条记录，19个特征，26.5%流失率，训练集通过SMOTE平衡至50%）上对比四种分类器：逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM。使用双层解释框架：全局SHAP排序和实例级SHAP与LIME分解。设计四层CRM集成架构，将风险分数和归因向量转换为分层细分，将关键特征映射到结构化留存行动模板，并将活动结果路由至重训练反馈循环。

Result: 逻辑回归获得最高AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM达到最高准确率（78.42%）；四种模型AUC差异在0.011范围内（0.831-0.841），五折交叉验证证实领先模型表现相当。全局SHAP分析识别出tenure、total charges和月度合约是主要流失信号。针对最高风险五分位数客户的策略预计可降低整体流失率3.3-5.3个百分点，每活动周期保留约19.9万-31.9美元。

Conclusion: 本研究成功弥合了高性能预测模型与实际CRM应用之间的差距，证明了结合可解释AI技术能够使不透明的预测模型在实际业务场景中落地，为电信运营商提供了可行的客户留存解决方案，实现了从模型预测到可操作干预策略的转化。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) reporting remains largely manual and time-consuming, and current EEG software ecosystems do not produce the structured EEG-text supervision needed for training modern language models. Most toolboxes focus on visualization or preprocessing, providing limited support for workflows that generate high-quality datasets for AI. We introduce EEG-to-Report, a browser-based annotation and feature-text framework that links routine EEG review with the construction of AI-ready datasets. The framework integrates multi-format EEG ingestion, channel standardization, and an interactive viewer with a multimodal annotation layer that combines typed text and transcribed voice notes. For each annotated segment, a feature extraction engine computes a standardized set of spectral, temporal, entropy, Hjorth, connectivity, and spike-related descriptors, stored alongside clinical descriptions in a portable JSON schema. This yields aligned feature-text pairs designed to supervise multimodal EEG-language models. The framework also includes an auto-report module that couples an ensemble of convolutional networks with a large language model to draft clinical narratives for neurologist review. Using pilot annotations, we describe how EEG-to-Report streamlines annotation workflows and produces editable draft reports, providing a reusable foundation for automated EEG reporting systems.

</details>


### [32] [Selection Bias Correction in Retail Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26156)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过蒙特卡洛模拟发现，在零售长尾分布的通胀估计中，分层方法优于逆概率加权（IPW），尤其在违反正定性假设时，分层误差仅0.008pp，而IPW达6.06pp；但在多项式平滑关系下，IPW表现更好，误差为0.007pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售情报常监测热门高流速商品，忽视利基商品的长尾，可能导致通胀估计的选择偏差，进而影响经济指标的准确性。

Method: 采用400次蒙特卡洛模拟，覆盖四种数据生成过程（对齐阶梯函数、平滑梯度、错位断点、多项式关系），对比逆概率加权（IPW）五种规格与不同分层数量下的分层方法。

Result: 分层方法在四种场景中三种表现优越，即使边界与总体断点刻意错位，误差仍低于0.04pp，相较IPW有116倍优势；但在平滑多项式关系下，IPW的样条倾向模型误差为0.007pp，优于分层的0.013pp。关键发现：即使具备完美结构知识的oracle IPW，在阶梯函数场景中误差达6.06pp，而分层仅为0.008pp，原因在于违反了因果推断中的正定性假设。

Conclusion: 在存在严重正定性违规的零售长尾分布中，分层方法是更安全的工程选择；IPW在平滑关系下有效，但在选择概率极端差异时超出其理论设计范围。

Abstract: Retail intelligence often relies on monitoring popular, high-velocity products, potentially biasing economic indicators by ignoring the "long tail" of niche items. This simulation study investigates selection bias in inflation estimation and compares correction methods across diverse data-generating processes. Through 400 Monte Carlo replications spanning four scenarios--aligned step functions, smooth gradients, misaligned breaks, and polynomial relationships--we test the robustness of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) with five specifications against stratification with varying strata counts. Our findings reveal fundamental limits of weighting methods in retail long-tail contexts: stratification achieves superior performance in three of four scenarios, maintaining sub-0.04pp median error even when boundaries deliberately misalign with population breaks (116x advantage over IPW). However, IPW with spline propensity models wins under smooth polynomial relationships (median error 0.007pp vs. 0.013pp), demonstrating context-dependency. Critically, even an oracle IPW specification with perfect structural knowledge achieves 6.06pp error compared to stratification's 0.008pp in step-function scenarios. This reflects violation of the Positivity Assumption--a fundamental causal inference requirement--rather than IPW methodological inferiority. When selection probabilities differ dramatically (90% vs. 1%), weighting methods operate outside their theoretical design envelope. These results demonstrate that stratification provides a safer engineering choice in retail long-tail distributions with severe positivity violations.

</details>


### [33] [GROUND: Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-Based Enterprise Analytics Through Governed Semantic Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26157)
*Aravind Sasidharan Pillai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GROUND框架通过引入受治理的语义层，解决了企业数据仓库自然语言分析中的幻觉指标、无效连接、错误粒度、不安全数据访问和缺乏解释等问题，实现了零幻觉和零行级安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本转SQL系统仅基于数据库模式或检索文档进行生成，缺乏企业报表所需的受治理业务语义（批准的指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器和行级安全），导致生产环境中出现幻觉指标、无效连接、错误粒度、不安全数据访问和缺乏解释等问题。

Method: 提出GROUND框架，将LLM生成的分析约束到受治理的语义层：提供批准的定义，将用户意图绑定到受治理的指标和维度，在执行前根据模式、指标、连接、粒度、过滤器、安全和成本规则验证生成的SQL，违规时重试或拒绝执行。

Result: 在100个问题的合成企业报表基准测试中，GROUND是唯一在所有六个评估类别中零幻觉的系统，而未受治理的系统在许多问题上违反行级安全。仅有语义定义但无访问策略的条件仍会泄露数据。在美国NHTSA车辆安全真实数据上的实验复现了这些发现，在三个提供商的四个模型上测试，GROUND的强制保证（特别是过滤器和行级安全）在所有模型上保持零违规。

Conclusion: 治理不能仅靠指标保真度实现，必须通过受治理的语义层来强制执行安全策略。GROUND证明了通过受治理的语义约束可以有效消除幻觉和安全违规，但依赖判断的行为（如拒绝未定义指标）仍存在易错性。

Abstract: Natural-language analytics over enterprise data warehouses is increasingly important, but production use is limited by hallucinated metrics, invalid joins, wrong grain, unsafe data access, and unsupported explanations. Existing text-to-SQL systems often ground generation in database schemas or retrieved documentation, while enterprise reporting also requires governed business semantics: approved metrics, dimensions, join paths, filters, and row-level security. This paper introduces GROUND, Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions, a framework that constrains LLM-generated analytics to a governed semantic layer. GROUND supplies approved definitions, binds user intent to governed metrics and dimensions, and validates generated SQL against schema, metric, join, grain, filter, security, and cost rules before execution. On violations, it retries or abstains.
  In a 100-question synthetic enterprise-reporting benchmark, GROUND is compared with direct schema-only text-to-SQL, schema-RAG, and semantic-only grounding under one shared model. GROUND is the only system free of measured hallucinations across all six evaluated categories, while ungoverned systems violate row-level security on many questions. A semantic-only condition with exact metric definitions but no access policy still leaks data, showing that governance cannot be replaced by metric fidelity alone. The findings are replicated on real U.S. NHTSA vehicle-safety data with independent hand-authored gold and tested on an adversarial set across four models from three providers. GROUND's enforced guarantees, especially filters and row-level security, hold with zero violations on every model, while judgment-dependent behaviors such as refusing undefined metrics remain fallible.

</details>
